1 --- ( 1 ) अधिसूचित करना कि जूट, सूत और जूट सुमली का निर्यात में 
पूर्व स्वालिटी नियंत्रण पौर निरीक्षण किया जाएगा । 

( 2 ) इम मादेश के उपाबंध में दिए गए जूट प्रारूप मून और शूट 
सुनाली निर्यात ( बालिटी नियंत्रण पौर निरीक्षण ) नियम 1882 के 
अनुसार सवालिटी मिक्षण पोर निरीक्षण के प्रकार को क्वालिटी मिवंग 
और निरीक्षण के ऐसे प्रकार के रूप में विनिर्दिष्ट करना जो निर्यात में 
पूर्व ऐसे जूट सूत या जूट सुतली को लागू होगा । 

( 3 ) जूट सूत और जूट सुतमी निर्यात (निरीक्षण ) नियम , 1982 
के नियम 3 में प्रधियित अपेक्षामो के अनुपालन के प्रधीम रहते हुए 
संविदात्मक विनिर्देशों को जूट सून पौर जूट सुतली के लिए मानक विनिर्देशों 
के रूप में मान्यता देना । । 

( 4 ) अन्तर्राष्ट्रीय व्यापार के अनुक्रम में जूट सूत और जूट सुसली 
के निर्यात की तब तक प्रतिषिद्ध करना जब तक कि उनके साथ निर्यात 
( क्वालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण ) अधिनियम , 1963 ( 1963 का 22 ) 
की धारा 7 के अधीन स्थापित निर्याप्त निरीक्षण अभिकरणो में से 
किसी एक के द्वारा जारी किया गया इस प्राशय का प्रमाण-पत्र 
म हो कि जूट सूत पौर जूट मुतली उपपैरा ( 3 ) के अधीन माम्पत्ता 
प्राप्त विनिर्देशों के प्रमुगप है और निर्यात योग्य है । 

2 - इस प्रदेश की कोई भी बात भावी क्रेताओं को समुद्र , भूमि 
या वायु मार्ग क्वारा जूट सूत और जूट सुतली के नमूनों के निर्यात पर 
लागू नहीं होगी परन्तु यह सब जब कि ऐसे एफ या अधिक मभूनों का पोत 
पर्यन्त निःशुल्क मूल्य 100 रुपए ( एक सौ रुपए ) से अधिक नहीं है ।

लड़ियो को एक माय बस्फर, मनाई गयी जूट सुतली अभिप्रेत है ।

11. Appeal.- ( 1 ) Any person aggi ieved by the refusal of 
the agency to issue a certificate under sub - rule ( 4 ) of rule 
6 or rule 7 as the case may be, may, within ten days of 
the receipt of the communication of such refusal, prefer an 
appeal to panel of experts consisting of not less than three 
poisons, as may be copstituted for the purpose by the Cen 
tral Government. 

(2 ) The quorum of the panel of experts shall be three 

( 3 ) The decision of the panel of experts on such appeal 
shall be final. 

( 4 ) The appeal shall be disposed of within fifteen days of 
its receipt .

निर्यात ( क्वालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण ) नियम , 1982 के 
अनसार निरीक्षण के प्रकार को ऐसे निरीक्षण के प्रकार के रूप 
में विनिर्दिष्ट करना जो निर्यात से पूर्व ऐमी हल्दी पर लाग 
होगा । 

( 3) समय- समय पर संशोधित किए गए हल्दी के श्रेणी 
करण और चिन्हांकन नियम , 1984 के अधीन बनाए गए श्रेणी 
अभिधानों को , हल्दी के मानक विनिर्देशों के रा म मान्र ता 
देना । 

( 4) अर्न्तराष्ट्रीय व्यापार के दौरान , हल्दी के निर्यात को 
तब तक प्रतिषिध्द करना , जब तक कि केन्द्रीय सरकार 
द्वारा मानता प्राप्त चिन्ह या सील ऐसे हल्दी के पैकेजों और 
डिब्बों पर चिपकाई या लगाई नही गई है कि वह उस पर 
लाग मानक विनिर्देशो के अनुरूप है और उसके साथ भारत 
सरकार के कृषि विपणन सलाहकार या इसके द्वारा इस 
निमिस्त प्राधिकृत कोई अधिकारी या राजपत्र में अधिसूचना 
के द्वारा केन्द्रीय सरकार किसी भी अधिमूचित अभिकरण 
द्वारा जारी किया गया श्रेणीकरण का प्रमाण - पत्र इसकी योग्यता 
के योग्यतर के प्रमाण स्वरूप नही लगा है । 

3 . इस आदेश की कोई भी बात भावी ऋताओं को भूमि , 
जल या वायु मार्ग द्वारा हल्वी के चाम झपये से अधिक 
मूल्य के नमूनों के निर्यात को लाग नही होगी । 

4 . इस आदेश में हल्दी से भारत में उत्पादित कर्कमा 
लोंग एल पधि का हल्दी प्रकन्द अभिप्रत है ।

( 4 ) To prohibit the export in the course of International 
trade of Turmeric unless a mark or seal recognised by the 
Central Government indicating that it conforms to the 
standard specifications applicable to it has been affixed or 
applied to packages or containers of stich Turmeric , and is 
accompanied by a certificate of grading issued by the Agri 
cultural Marketing Adviser to the Government of India or 
by an Officer authorised by him in this behalf or any other 
Agency notified by the Central Government by notification 
in the Official Gazette in token of its exportworthiness . 

3 . Nothing in this order shall apply to export by sea , lond 
or air of samples of Turmeric not exceeding in value of 
rupees fifty to prospective buyers . 

4 . In this order Turmeric means the Turmeric rhizomes 
of the Plant Curcuma Longa L produced in India . 

___ ANNEXURE-I 
DRAFT RULES PROPOSED TO BE MADE UNDER 
SECTION 17 OF THE EXPORT (QUALITY CONTROL 

AND INSPECTION) ACT , 1963 (22 OF 1963 ) 
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 17 of the 
Export ( Quality, Control and Inspection ) Act, 1963 ( 22 of 
1963 ), the Central Government, horoby, makes the follow 
ing rules, namely : 

1 . Short title and commencement. ( 1 ) These rules may 
be called the Export of Turmeric (Inspection ) Rules, 1982 . 

2 . They shall come into force on the date of their publi 
cation in the official Gazette . 


2. Definitions . -- In these rules , unless tha context other 
wise requires 


( a) Act means the Export ( Quality Control and Ins 

pection) Act, 1963 (22 of 1963) : 


(b ) Agricultural Markeung Advisor means the Agri 

cultural Marketing Adviser to the Government of 
India%3B 


( c) Inspecting Officer means the officer authorised by 

the Agricultural Marketing Adviser for inspection 
of Turmeric or any other Agency recognised as the 
Agency under Soction 7 of the Act ; 


S. O . 1524. .- Whereas in exercise of the powers conferred 
by Section 6 of the Export ( Quality Control and Inspection ) 
Act, 1963 ( 22 of 1963) the Central Government s of the 
opinion that it is necessary and expedient so to do for the 
development of the export trade of India that Turmeric 
should be subject to quality control and inspection prior to 
export : 

And whereas the Central Government has formulated the 
proposals specified below for the said purposc and has for 
warded the same to the Export Inspection Council, as ro 
quired by sub- rule ( 2 ) of rule 11 of the Export ( Quality 
Control and Inspection) Rules, 1964 : 


( d ) authorised Packer means the person or body of 

persons who has been granted & Certificate of 
Authorisation by tho Agricultural Marketing Advl 
ser to the Government of India or any other Agency 
recognised under section 7 of the Act for getting 
the commodity graded and marked in accordance 
with the grade standard procedure prescribed under 
the rules , 


( e ) Certificate of Authorisation means the certificate 

issued by the Agricultural Marketing Adviser or 
any officer authorisod in this behalf or any other 
Agency recognised under section 7 of the Act to a 
porson or body of persons, desirous of grading Tur 
meric (recognised under section 6 of the Act ). 


Now , therefore, in pursuance of the said sub- rule: the 
Contral Government hereby publishes the said proposals for 
the information of the public likely to be affected theroby . 

2. Notice is hereby given that any person douiring to for 
Ward any objections or suggestions with respect to the sald 
proposals may forward the samo within fortyfive days of the 
date of the publication of this order in the Official Gazette 
to the Export Inspection Agency - Cochin , Manohar , M . G . 
Road, COCHIN- 682011 . 


( f) Turmeric means the Turmeric Thizories of the plant 

Curcuma Longa L produced in India . 


PROPOSALS 
( 1) To notify that Turmeric shall be subject to quality 
control and inspection prior to export ; 


3 . Basis of Inspection - Inspection of Turmeric intended 
for export shall be carried out with a view to seeing that 
the same conforms to the standard specifications recognisec 
by the Central Government under section 6 of the Act ; 
. 4. Procedure of Inspection. -- (1) Turmeric for export shal 
be graded and packed only by the authorised packer in ac 
cordance with the instructions isslied in this behalf by the 
Agricultural Marketing Adviser or any other Agency recog 
nised under section 7 of the Act, 


( 2 ) To specify the type of inspection in Bocordance with 
the draft Export of Turmeric (Inspection ) Rules, 1982 , set 
out in the Annexure I to this Order , Ag the type of Inspec 
tion which shall apply to guch Turmeric prior to export. 


( 3 ) To recognise the grade designation formulated under 
the Turmeric Grading and Marking Rules, 1964 as amended 
from time to time, as the standard specifications for puch 
Turmeric . 


(2 ) It shall be the responsibility of the authorised packe 
to make such arrangements for grading and sampling o 
Turmeric and also provide requisite facilities for testing etc 
as may be presribed by the Agricultural Marketing Advise 
or any other Agency rccognised under Section 7 of th 
Act.

section 7 of the Art, at any place before export. 

6 . Payment of charges for Labels . - - The authorised packer 
shall pay the label charges which are notified by the Govern 
ment of India from time to time, in the manner specified 
by the Agricultural Marketing Adviser or any other Agency 
rccognised under Section 7 of the Act. 


S . O . 1325. — Whereas the Central Government, in exer 
cive of the powers conferred by Section 8 of the Export 
(Quality Control and Inspection ) Act, 1963 ( 22 of 1963), 
proposes to recognise the grade designation mark described 
under Rule 5 of the Turmeric Grading and Marking Rules, 
1964 with respect to Turmeria for the purpose of denoting 
that where packages or containers containing Turmeric are 
affixed with the prescribed labely, the Turmeric in such pack 
ages or containers conform to the standard specifications 
applicable thereto under clause ( W ) of section 6 of the Act. 


And whereas the Central Government has forwarded the 
aforesaid proposals to the Export Inspection Council as re 
quired by sub- rule ( 2 ) of rule 11 of the Export (Quality 
Control and Inspection ) Rules, 1964 ;

मा०मा० 1535 ..यत. इस सलग्न अनुसूची में विमिविष्ट और पेट्रो 
लियम खनिज पाइपलाईन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकारों का प्रर्जन ) 
मधिनियम , 1962 की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन प्रकाशित 
भारत मरकार की अधिसूचना द्वारा इण्डियन प्रायल झारिशन लिमिटेड 
के लिए उत्तर प्रदेश मे मधुग से पजाब में जलन्धर तक पेट्रोलियम के 
परिवहन के लिए उस मलान अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियो के उपयोग का 
मधिकार पजिन पर लिया गया है । 

पौर यत. इण्डियन प्रायन कार्पोरेशन लिमिटेड ने उक्त प्रधिनियम की धाग 
7 की उपधाग ( 1 ) के स्रोण ( 1 ) में मिविष्ट प्रक्रिया को अनुसूची में 
निर्दिष्ट गांव के नाम के मामने दिखाई गई तिथि में पर्यवमित कर दिया है ।

का०मा० 1537.-.--यतः इस मंलग्न अनसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट और 
पेट्रोलियम खनिज पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकारों का मर्जन ) 
अधिनियम , 1962 की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन प्रकाशित 
भारत सरकार की अधिसूचना द्वारा इणियन पायल कार्पोरेशन लिमिटेड 
के लिए उत्तर प्रदेश में मथुरा से पंजाब में जालन्धर तक पेट्रोलियम के 
परिवहन के लिए उस संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियों के उपयोग 
का अधिकार अजित कर लिया गया हैं । 

पौर यतः इण्डियन प्रॉयल कार्पोरेशन लिमिटेड ने उक्त अधिनियम को 
धारा 7 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( 1 ) में निविष्ट प्रक्रिया को अनुसूची 
में निर्दिष्ट गांव के नाम के सामने दिखाई गई तिथि से पर्यवसित कर 
दिया है । 
___ अब यतः पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइपलाइन ( भूमि के उपयोग के 
अधिकारों का प्रजन ) नियमावली, 1963 के नियम 4 के अधीन सकाम 
प्राधिकारी उक्त तिथि को ऊपर निविष्ट संक्रिया पर्यवमान के रूप में 
एतद्वारा अधिसूचित करते हैं ।

टिप्पण ? -- कोयला धारक क्षेत्र ( पर्जन पार विकास ) अधिनियम , 
1957 ( 1957 का 20 ) की धारा 8 के उपबग्धों की मोर ध्यान 
भाकृष्ट किया जाता है जिसमें निम्नलिखित उपबन्धित : 


" 8 ( 1 ) कोई व्यक्ति जो किसी भूमि में जिसकी पागत धारा 7 के 

अधीन अधिसूचना, निकाली गई है, हितवर है, अधिसूचना 
के निकाले जाने से तीस दिन के भीतर सम्पूर्ण भूमि या 
उसके किसी भाग या ऐसी भूमि में या उस पर किन्ही 
अधिकारों का अर्जन किये जाने के बारे में भापत्ति कर सकेगा । 


स्पष्टीकरण - इस धारा के प्रान्तर्गत यह प्रापत्ति नहीं मानी जायेगी 
कि कोई व्यक्ति किसी भूमि में कोयला उत्पादन के लिये स्वयं खनन 
संक्रियाएं करना चाहता है और ऐसी संक्रियाएं केन्द्रीय सरकार या किसी 
मन्य व्यक्ति को नहीं करना चाहिये । 


( 2 ) उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन प्रत्येक मापत्ति सक्षम प्राधिकारी 
को लिखित रूप में की जायेगी और सक्षम प्राधिकारी मापत्तिकर्ता को 
स्वयं सने जामे का या विधि व्यवसायी द्वारा सुनवाई का अवसर देगा 
पौर ऐसी सभी मापत्तियों को सुनने के पश्चात् पौर ऐसी प्रतिरिक्त 
जाँच , दि कोई है , करने के पश्चात् जो वह प्रावश्यक समझता है यह 
या तो धार। 7 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के प्रधीन अधिसूचित भूमि के मा 
ऐसी भूमि मे या उस पर अधिकारों के संबंध में एक रिपोर्ट या ऐसी 
भमि के विभिम टुकड़ों या सी भूमि में या उस पर के अधिकारों के 
संबंध में आपत्तियों पर अपनी सिफारिशों मोर उसके द्वारा की गई कार्य 
बाही के अभिलेख सहित विभिन्न रिपोर्ट केन्द्रीय सरकार को उसके या 
विभिश्चय के लिये देगा ।

Objection to acquisition - .. 
" 8 ( 1 ) Any person interested in any land in respect of 

which a notification under section 7 has been issued 
may , within thirty days of the issue of the notifica 
tion , object to the acquistion of the whole or any

land . 
Explanation . It shall not be an objection within the 

meaning of this section for any person to say that 
he himself desires to undertake mining operations 
in the land for the production of coal and that 
such operations should not be undertaken by the 
Central Government or by any other person. 
Every objection under sub -section ( 1 ) shall bo 
mrade to the competent authority in writing, and the 
competent authority shall give the objector an 
opportunity of being heard either in person or by 
a legal practitioner and shall, after hearing all such 
objections and after making such further inquiry , if 
any , as he thinks necessary, cither make a report in 
respect of the land which has been notified under 
sub -section ( 1 ) of section 7 Or of rights in or 
over such land or nake different reports in respect 
of different parcels of such land or of rights in or 
over such fand to the Central Government, con 
taīning his recommendations on the objections, 
together with the record of the proceedings held hy 
htm , for the decision of that Government.

S . O . 1543 . — -Whereay in pursuance of the notification of 
the Government of India in the late Ministry of Steel, 
Mines and Fuel (Department of Mines and Fuel ) No . S . O . 
702 dated the 18th March , 1960 under section 9 of the Ccal 
Dcoring Areas (Acquisition and Development) Act , 1957 
( 20 of 1957 ) , the Central Government has acquired the 
lands measuring 998 .90 acres (approx.) or 404.19 hectares 
( upprox. ) in villagc Balanda , Dera and Ghantapara Thana 
Colliery P . S ., district Dhenkanal ( Orişya ). 

- And whereas Butu Pradhan Son of Sirchina Pradhan of 
village Balanda P . O . Dera Colliery , District Dhenkanal 
(Osissa ) , the person interested har under section 13 of 
the said Act, preferred his claim for compensation for acquisi 
tion of his land measuring 1,00 acros or 0.405 hectare which 
forms part of the said lande so acquired , before the Compe 
tent Authority :

[ No. 13 ( 5) / 80- CL] 


मार्ष, 1960 के अनुसरण में , ग्राम बालम्बा हेरा और घंटापारा थाना 
कोलियरी, जिला धेनकानल ( उड़ीसा ) में 998. 90 एकर ( लगभग ) या 
404. 19 हैक्टर ( लगभग ) भूमि अर्जित कर ली है 
___ और ग्राम डेरा डाकघर छेरा कोलियरी, जिला धेनकानल ( उड़ीमा ) के 
सर्व श्री सौरी प्रधान फिरतन प्रधान, बनका प्रधान पुत्र श्री डांगी 
प्रधान ने , जो हितंबद्ध व्यक्ति है, अधिनियम की धारा 13 के अधीन , 0 . 08 
एका या 0 . 324 हैक्टर माप की अपनी भूमि के, जो इस प्रकार अजित 
भूमि का भाग है, अर्जन के प्रतिकर के लिए सक्षम प्राधिकारी के समक्ष 
अपना दावा किया है । 


मौर उक्त प्रर्जन के लिए देय प्रप्तिकर की रकम, प्रस्थापित प्रतिकर की 
रकम प्राप्त करने के लिए हम . का निर्याधन न होने के कारण , करार द्वार , 
नियत नहीं की जा सकी । 

अतः, केन्द्रीय सरकार, उक्त अधिनियम की धार, 14 की उपधारा 
( 2 ) द्वारा प्रवत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, इस दावेदार को संदेय 
प्रतिफर की रकम प्राप्त करने के हक की घोषणा करने के प्रयोजन के 
लिए एक प्रधिकरण गठित करती है, जिसमें जिला न्यायाधीश, धेनकानल 
( उड़ीसा ) अंशकालिक अधिकरण होंगे । 

[ सं० 13 ( 8 )/ 80-सी० एल०] 

स्वर्ण सिह, अवर सचिव 


का० आ० 1544 .- - केन्द्रीय सरकार ने , कोयला धारक क्षेत्र ( अर्जन 
और विकाम ) अधिनियम 1957 ( 1967 का 20 ) की धारा 9 के अधीन , 
भारत सरकार के भूतपूर्व इस्पात, खान और धन मंत्रालय ( खान ) और 
इंधन विभाग ) की अधिसूचना सं० का० प्रा० 702 तारीख 18 मार्च , 
1980 के अनुसरण में , ग्राम पालन्या डेरा और पटापारा थाना कोलियरी , 
जिला घेनकानल ( उड़ीमा ) में 998 . 90 एकर ( लगभग ) या 404 . 10 
हेक्टर ( लगभग ) भूमि अर्जित कर ली है ; 

और प्राम बालग्या , डाकघर डेरा कोलियरी , जिल , धेनकानल ( उड़ीसा ) 
के श्री मकुन्द साहू पौर भगवान साहू पुत्र श्री नीला माह ने , जो हितबद्ध 
व्यक्ति है, अधिनियम की धारा 13 के अधीन , 0 .04 एकड़ या 0 . 162 
हैक्टर माप की अपनी भूमि के , जो इस प्रकार अजित भूमि का भाग है , 
मर्जन के प्रतिकर के लिए सक्षम प्राधिकारी के समक्ष अपना दावा किया है , 
__ और उपत प्रर्जन के लिए देय प्रतिकर की रकम , प्रस्थापित प्रतिफर 
की रकम प्राप्त करने के लिए हक का निर्वाधन न होने के कारण , करार 
द्वारा नियत नहीं की जा सकी । 

प्रप्तः , केन्द्रीय सरकार , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 14 की उपधारा 
( 2 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए , इस वावेदार को संवेय 
प्रतिकर की रकम प्राप्त करने के हक की घोषणा करने के प्रयोजन के 
लिए एक अधिकरण गठित करती है , जिसमें जिला न्यायाधीश, धेनकानल 
( उड़ीसा ) अंशकालिक अधिकरण होगे ।

का० मा० 1553. -- केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि इससे उपाय 
प्रमुसूची में पिनिविष्ट विषय के बारे में एस० सी० मंपनी लि . गोवावरी 
खानी से संबंड एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों के 
बीच विद्यमान है; 

भौर केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को म्यायनिर्णयम के लिए निर्देशित 
करता गांछनीय समझती है। 


प्रता, केन्द्रीय सरकार, प्रायोगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 7- पोर धारा 10 को उम-धारा ( 1 ) के बंड 
( ष ) पारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, एक प्रोपोगिक मधिकरण 
गठित करती है जिसके पीठासीन अधिकारी श्री बी० प्रसाद राव होंगे , 
जिनका मुख्यालय हैदराबाद में होगा और उक्त विवाद को उस मधिकरण 
को म्यायनिर्णयन के लिए निशित करती है ।

मा०मा० 155 4. - -केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि इससे उपाय 
अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषय के मारे में मैसर्स सिंगरेनी कोलियरीज कम्पनी 
लि०, रामागुंडम डिवीजन -II के प्रबंधपत्र से सम्बस एक प्रौद्योगिक 
विवाद नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच विद्यमान है ; 

पौर केन्द्रीय सरकार उफ्न विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए निर्देशित 
करना वांछनीय समझती है । 

प्रत केन्द्रीय सरकार , प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम, 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 7-क और धारा 10 की उप-धारा ( 1 ) के बड 
द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, एक प्रौद्योगिक अधिकरण गठित 
करती है जिसके पीठासीन अधिकारी श्री बी० प्रसाद राव होगें , जिनका 
मुख्यालय हैदराबाद में होगा और उक्त विवाद को उक्त प्रधिकरण को 
म्य . यनिर्णयन के लिए निर्देशित करती है ।

" क्या मैसर्स सिगरेमी कोलियरीज कम्पनी लि ., रामागुन्हम क्षेत्र , 
डिवीजन - II के प्रबन्धसन्त्र की कार्रवाई : 
1. गोदावरी खानी के 18 एम जम्लयू० पावर हाऊस के स्विच 

गोर्ड परिपरो, सवश्री के . माधराव , के ० राजरेड्डी, जी बेकटेश्वरा 
रेड्डी , एन० जीरजनाचारी , बी० नरसिम्हैया , एन० भन्दैया, के . 
गाटेहा और जे०एस०पार० मूर्ति को श्रेणी VI “ग ग्रेड प्रदान 

न करने में ; 
2 सर्वश्री बी राजैया , वी० शंकर, शेख मस्तान , अब्दुल लतीफ , 

संयंत्र परिचरों को श्रेणी V प्रदान न करने में और बाद में 
श्रेणी IV में पदोन्नत हुए कम्बेमर खलासियो से कम मजदूरी 
प्राप्त करने वाले इन संयंत्र परिचरों को प्रतिरिक्त वेतनवृद्धियो 

को मंजूरी न देने में ;

It has become necessary to give retrospective effect to the 
व्याख्यास्मक ज्ञापन 

excmption in this case , as the processing of the application 

for exemption took time. However , it is certified that the 
इम मामले में पूर्वापेक्षी प्रभाव से छूट देनी आवश्यक हो गई है, क्योंकि 

grant of exemption with retrospoctive effect will not affect 
छूट के लिए प्राप्त आवेदन-पत्र की कार्रवाई पर समय लगा । तथापि , tho interest of anybody adversely.

New Delhi, the 1st April, 1982 
8 .0 . 1563. In pursuance of section 17 of the Industrial 
Disputos Act, 1947 ( 14 of 1947 ) , the Central Government 
hereby publishes the following award of the Central Gov 
ernment Industrial Tribunal, Jabalpur, in the Industrial Dis 
puto betwcen tho employer in relation to the management 
of Divisional Engineer, Coaxial Telephone, Nagpur , and their 
workman which was received by the Contral Government on 
the 27th March , 1982 


3. Similar notices were issued by the Labour Ministry 
as well as by this Tribunal to the Divisional Engineer , 
Coaxial Telephone , Nagpur. Tho Divisional Engincer appe 
ared through his representative, Shri C . R . Khadkar , Junior 
Englacer , and filled the statement of claim on his bohall . 

4 . In the absence of any statement of claim by. the 
workman the føcts , which give rise to this dispute as they 
appear from the statement of claim by the Divisional 
Epgineer, may bo stated as under : 

The workman , Shri Ramarao was a Casual Field Worker 
and he was frequently absenting from ble duties without 
sanction of any leave. From 7th March 1980 he was asked 
to report for duty at Wardha by the Junior Engineer, 
but he was found absent. Again on 14th March 1980 he 
did not report for duty at Nagpur . Subsequently from 
24th to 27th March , 1980 he similarly rerained absent. 
Thereafter from 1 to 14th April, 1980 he again remained 
absent. In the above mentioned circumstances . bacause 
of his frequent absenteism followed by continuod absence 
AS indicated above, his services had to be terminated be 
cause the workman was not a regular remp and was engaged 
only on a casual basis . 

5. As agalost this claim filed by the Divisional Engineer, 
the workman has neither appeared nor has filed his atate 
ment of clair . His continuod absence and omission to filo 
his statement is clearly indicative of the fact that he has 
no subsisting dispute with big ex -employer and he is not 
desirous of obtaining any relief from this Tribunal. If 
there is no dispute between the workman and the manage 
ment then there is no issue which requires ony adjudica 
tion by this Tribunal. 

6 . The Divisional Engineer has given reasons as to why 
the services of the workman were elther discontinued or 
terminated. The workman has not come forward with his 
claim that by virtue of working for any prescribed period 
ho had become a permanent or continuo119 workrran of the 
department. In these circumstances, the only inference would 
be that the workman does not want to prosocute this disputo 
and staterront made on behalf of the management should 
be accepted . Accordingly , for the reasons given , it is held 
that the action of the Divisional Engineer , Coaxial Telephone, 
Nagpur in terminating the services of the Casual Field 
Worker , Shri Ramarao Tulsiram Mato, from 1st April , 1980 
18 fully justified and the workman fo not entitled to any 
relief . The workmran has not appeared , The Divisional En 
gineer shall bear his own costs as incurred ,

11. In that iew of the matter if the concerned workman 
did not agree to do the surface work of a Wagon Loader 
the tranagement is not to be blamed and the work of a 
Trammer cannot be assigned to him . 

12. Thus on a consideration of the evidence on record and 
facts and circumstances of the case , I hold that as per proc 
ti e the concerned workman is entitled to be provided with 
work on the surface but he cannot demand any particular 
work on the surface . The management should offer him 
the work of a Wagon Loader or any other work of lighter 
type on the surface which the workman should accept. The 
workman is also entitled to his sick and leave wages which 
the management has agreed to pay . 

13 . The award is given accordingly.

( 2 ) Facts leading upto this dispute are as follows : The 
Annexure to the reference mado by the Government of India , 
Ministry of Labour deals with 44 and 15 workers employed 
respectively by Ms. C . K . Balasubramaniam , Handling 
Agents, 3, 2nd line Bosch , Madras. 1 and Ms. Westem 
Agencies, No. 192 -Linghi Chetty Street Madra6 - 1 . These two 
Managements were Contractors of Minerals and Metals Trad 
ing Corporation of India Ltd ., Channai House , Esplanado , 
Madras - 1 . MMTC was handling mineral inside the Madras 
Port from 1961 to 1976 through various contractors , includ 
ing Ms. C . K . Balasubramaniam and Ms, Western Agoncies, 
Madras . It is common case that from 1 - 2 -1976 the work 
relating to handling of Iron Oro was taken over by Madras 
Port Trust and consequently the workmen employed by the 
two contractorg referred to above were rendered without work , 
On behalf of M /s , Western Agencies, it is also pointed out 
that consequent on the stoppage of contract work by MMTC 
the workmen were paid all their legal dues on the closure 
of the business . W . W . 1 who was employed as a Supervisor 
under the Western Agencies and who is No. 15 of the work 
men of Western Agencies mentioned in the Annexuro to the 
reference made by the Government of India was obliged 
to admit in cross examination that the Company had paid 
Retrenchment compensation to all the employees , Ex . M . 1 
series would also confirm tho act that Ms, Western Agencies 
have disbursed retrenchment compensation etc ., as per agrec 
ment dated 9 - 2 - 1976 . Similarly it can also be taken that the 
workmen of Ms. C . K . Balasubramaniam were also paid 
their legal dues consequent on the stoppage of work , Thero 
is no dispute about the total years of service put in by the 
workmen respectively under the two contractors. The relief 
now sought for by these workmen is that from 1 - 2 - 1976 most 
of the retrenched workmen remained unemployed and there 
fort in as much as the entire work is carried on by the 
MMTC with the help of Madrag Port Trust, either tho

Calicut and others ) where it is pointed out that " If the

of Labour held a discussion in which tho Madras Port 
Prist Officials and Minerals and Metal Trading Corporation

have also participated for conditation proceedings. Ex .

Officer on 20th November ,, 80. Ex. W . 8 is the letter from 
the MMTC to tho conciliation Officer on 13th August 79. 
Ex W . 7 is the conciliation failure report dated 5th January 
81. It can be gathered from Ex . W . 7 that the representa 
tive of MMTC and Madras Port Trust pointed out that 
tbord are no responsibilities whātsoever and the MMTC 
Toiect on the letter of the Corrmerce Ministry , dated 1st 
June 1977 vide Ex. W . 3 to show that they cannot provide 
altornative employment to the workmen . While the Union was

chd not agree to it. Thus it is manifest that both Madras 
Port Trust and MMTC were perfectly aware of the demande 
takto by these workmen on them pending over several 
years and eventually . when the petitioner -Union moved the 
machinery of the Labour Department Central in con 
cllibtloh both Madras Port Tust and representatives of 
Madras Port Trust and MMTC actually participated . On 
these facts it can never be said that no demand 99 such 
were rade on these Managements and that the Manage 
tents wore not parties to the conciliation proceedings. 
Just because in the body of the reference made by the 
Government there is no whisper about the Madras Port 


Trust or MMTC it cannot be said that Madras Port Trust 
and MMTC are not parties as contoinplated under Section 18 
( 3 ) (a ) of the Industrial Dispute Act, 1947. It is now well 
settled that the Managements who participatod in the con 
ciliation proceedings must be decmed to be partics to 
the dispute raised by the workmen , that apart under Section 
18 ( 3 ) (b ) of the Industrial Disputege Act, 1947 yen parties 
summoned to appear in the proceeding as paities with the 
dispute will be bound by the Award . On the materials 
placed , by no stretch of imagination can it be said that 
either madras Port Trust or MMTC had been surmoned 
of this Tribunal without proper cause . In that view any 
award pa sed by this Tribunal would also be blading on 
Madras Port Trust and MMTC uso under Section S . 18 ( 3 ) 
( b ) of the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947. Apparently , although 
Madras Port Trust draws their labour requirement through 
the medium of Madras Labour Board in as much as tho 
Union did not look to Madras Labour Board directly for 
their employment at any stage and in as much as the Madras 
Dock Labour Board did not participate in the conciliation 
procecdings before the concerned Officer and in ag much 
as no summons have been issued to the Madras Dock 
Labour Board by this Tribunal either suo moto or at 
instance of the Petitioner -Union , Madras Dock Labour 
Board will not be directly bound by this Award . It is 
quite a different matter that the Madras Dock Labour 
Board cngages their workmen at the instance of either the 
Madras Port Trust or even MMTC . It is true that under 
paragraph ( 14 ) of the claim statement filed by the peti 
tioner- Union it is also prayed alternative that Dock 
Labour Board may be directed to give employment of 
these workmen , thc Learned authorised representative Thiru 
Bataram did not quite rightly press during his arguments 
this alternative relief. On the materials placed the relief 
granted by this Tribunal would be effective both against 
the Madras Port Trust and MMTC . It is stated at the bar 
that crrployment opportunities are limitless both under 
MMTC (Wholly owned by Government of India ) and 
Madras Port Trust. 

(6 ) In the result I find that there is no apparent justification 
for the non -employment of the workmen referred to in Annexure 
to the reference made by the Government of India with effect 
from 1 -2 - 1976 , 

(7 ) In the circumstances an Award is passed directing the 
Madras Port Trust and the Minerals & Metals Trading -Corpore 
tion of India Ltd ., to give employment to the workmen mentioned 
in the Annexure to the reference made by the Governmont of 
India in future as and when an Occasion arises. In the poculiar 
circumstances, I diroct the partics to bear their respectivo costs.

New Delhi, the 8th April , 1982 . 
$ . 0 . 1568. - - In pursuance of section 17 of the Industrial 
Disputes Act, 1947 ( 14 of 1947) , the Central Govern 
ment hereby publishes the following award of the Central 
Government industrial Tribunal, Madras , in the industrial 
dispute between the employers in relation to the management 
of Central Bank of India and their workman , which was 
reccived by the Central Government on the 3 -4 - 1982 . 
BEFORE THIRU T. SUDARSANAM DANIEL , B .A ., B .L., 

PRESIDING OFFICER , 
INDUSTRIAL TRIBUNAL , TAMIL NADU . 
( Constituted by the Government of India ) 

Monday , 29th day of March , 1982. 
INDUSTRIAL DISPUTE NO . 12 OF 1982 
(In the matter of the dispute for adjudication under 

Section 10 ( 1 ) (d ) of the Industrial Dispute Act. 


AWARD 


This is an Industrial Dispute between the workmen and 
the Management of Central Bank of India , Madras referred 
to this Tribunal for adjudication under Section 10 ( 1 ) ( d ) 
of the Industrial Disputes Act , 1947 by the Government 
of India in their Order No, L - 12012 / 98 /81- D . II ( A ) , dated 
the 3rd February , 1982 in respect of the following issue : 


" Whether the Management of Central Bank of India , 

Madras is justified in demoting Smt. S . Palapiam 
mal Assistant Cashier - cum -Godown Kecper from 
the post of Assistant Head Cashier , a post carry 
ing special allowancc , with effect from 14 - 12 - 1979 ? 

If not, to wbat relief the said workman is entitled ? " 
( Z ) Partles were served with surrmons. 


( 3 ) At the request of Union s representative, the dispute 
was posted to 29- 3 - 1982 for filing claim statement. 


(4 ) Today , when the dispute was called for filling claim 
statement , the General Secretary of Union represented that 
the employee concerned in this dispute is not interested in 
pursuing the dispute and hence the dispute may be closed 
and he made endorsement to that effect. 


( 3 ) In vicw of the endorsement, award is passed bolding 
that the worker is not entitled to any relief. No costs . 

Dated , this 29th day of March , 1982 . 


T , SUDARSANAM DANIEL , Presiding Officer
